## Document 032: 2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更 
～銀行金利市場化の長い道のり～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹 
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 人民銀がリバースレポ翌日物の日常オペを開始。短期金利の誘導精度を高める狙い 
中国の中央銀行に当たる中国人民銀行（PBOC）が10月30日、市場に短期流動性を供給する「売り戻
し条件付き債券購入（リバースレポ）」翌日物の公開市場操作（オペ）を11月2日より毎営業日実施す
ると発表した。これまで原則として毎営業日実施していたリバースレポ7日物から切り替える。翌日物
のオペは固定金利、数量入札の方式で行い、その金利を政策金利と位置づける。インターバンク市場
のレポ取引では翌日物が大半を占めていることから、政策金利を7日物から翌日物に変更することで
期間のズレを解消し、短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙いがある。新たなオペ初日の金利は1.25％
であった（前営業日のリバースレポ7日物の金利は1.40％）。 
この動きは、PBOCの潘功勝行長（総裁）が10月19日の講演で明らかにしていた金融政策運営の改革
方針を実行に移したものである。潘氏は「銀行間債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）は事実上、中
央銀行の金融政策が調節する目標金利となっているが、それと同時に、中央銀行の公開市場操作の主
要な種類と主要な政策金利の期限はなお7日で、両者は完全には一致しない」ことを認めた上で、「適
切なタイミングで公開市場の主要な操作種類の期限を翌日物に切り替え、公開市場の翌日物レポ金利
を主要な政策金利とすることを検討している」と予告していた。この切り替えによって、PBOCの金融
政策運営は米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）や日本銀行のそれに近づくことになる。 
さらに、潘氏は銀行貸出金利の改革を進めていることも明らかにした。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）を
参照して貸出金利を決定するこれまでの運用に加え、DR001などの短期市場金利を直接参照して金利
を決定する方法を試験的に導入したのである。今後、財務力のある企業は商業銀行と協議の上でDR001
もしくはLPRを参照した貸出金利を選択することができ、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを参照
した金利を適用するとしている。経済誌『財新』によれば、2026年7月以降にDR001を参照した銀行貸
出がすでに全国で100件以上実施されているという。 
PBOCが新たな銀行貸出金利の決定メカニズムを模索する背景には、LPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、
貸出金利の実勢を十分に反映できていないことがあるとみられる。これは、2013年7月に下限規制が撤
廃される前の貸出基準金利が基準の70％まで引き下げを許容していた歴史的経緯から、信用力の高い
顧客にLPR以下の金利で貸出を実行する慣行が残っているためでもある。潘氏は改革を進めると同時
に、すでに実務的な役割を終えている貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を正式に廃止するとした。 
2026 年 11 月 9 日

### Page 2

2

■ 貸出基準金利から最優遇貸出金利へ移行。ただ、実勢金利は「最優遇」から乖離 
PBOCは2010年代以降、銀行金利の市場化改革を慎重に推し進めてきた（図表1）。改革以前はPBOC
が貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を直接コントロールし、商業銀行がその上下一定の範囲内で貸出・預
金金利を決定する制度となっていたが、PBOCは2013年7月に貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃し、同年10月
には全国銀行間コール市場センター（全国銀行間同業拆借中心。以下「コール市場センター」）を通
じて貸出基礎金利（旧LPR）の発表を開始した。旧LPRは、主要9行（のち10行）の報告銀行がコール
市場センターに貸出の最優遇金利を報告し、センター側がそのうち最高値と最低値を除いた報告金利
を報告銀行の貸出残高で加重平均して算出するものであった。 
PBOCは銀行貸出金利が市場原理に基づき決定されるメカニズムへの移行を意図していたが、PBOC
が引き続き基準金利を操作して利下げを行い、報告銀行も基準金利を強く意識して最優遇金利を報告
したため、旧LPRは貸出基準金利に張り付いて変動した。PBOCは「銀行が貸出を実行する際、多くが
なお貸出基準金利を参照して金利を決定し、特に一部の銀行が協調行動を通じて貸出基準金利の一定
図表1 銀行金利の市場化改革 
年月 No. 改革内容 
2004/10  銀行貸出金利の上限規制と銀行預金金利の下限規制を撤廃 
2013/7  ① 銀行貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の下限は貸出基準金利の 70％） 
2013/10 ② 貸出基礎金利（旧 LPR）の発表を開始 
2015/10 ③ 銀行預金金利の上限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の上限は預金基準金利の 150％） 
2019/8 ④ 貸出市場報告金利（新 LPR）の発表を開始。中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）金利を参照金利に採用 
2024/7 [1] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを開始（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の▲0.2％と＋0.5％） 
 ⑤ リバースレポ 7 日物金利を LPR の参照金利に位置づけ 
2026/6 [2] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを変更（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の±0.25％） 
2026/11 ⑥ リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを開始。その金利を政策金利に位置づけ

倍率（例えば0.9倍）を隠れた下限金利として設定しており、市場金利が実体経済に伝達することを妨
げている」と指摘している。 
そこで、PBOCは2019年8月、貸出市場報告金利（新LPR）と呼ばれる新たなメカニズムを導入する。
中長期流動性の供給ツールである中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）の金利を参照し、銀行がこれに資金
調達コストや市場需給、リスクプレミアムを反映した最優遇金利をコール市場センターに報告する方
法である。報告銀行は中小銀行を追加して18行（現在20行）まで拡大し、算出方法も加重平均から単
純平均に変更したことで、算出結果がウェイトの高い大手行の報告金利に引っ張られる傾向を排し、
指標としての包括性も高まった。この改革により、LPRは政策金利であるMLF1年物の金利と連動する
ようになったが、LPRが「最優遇」金利から乖離した状態は依然として解消されなかった。報告銀行
が旧LPRの時と同様にMLF金利を強く意識して金利を報告したためと考えられる。 
図表3は新LPRと実際の銀行貸出金利との関係を見たものである。LPRを下回る金利での貸出比率が
年々高まっていることが分かる。LPR1年物は2019年8月～2026年10月までの間に計▲1.25％Pt引き下げ
られているが、実勢金利は資金需要の低迷もあってそれ以上に下がっていることを示唆している。 
一方、銀行預金金利は2015年に上限規制を撤廃した後も、銀行間の過度な預金獲得競争を防ぐため
窓口指導や業界の自主規制の形をとった上限管理を維持し、2022年4月になって10年物国債利回りや
LPR1年物を参照して金利を決定するメカニズムを導入した。上限管理が廃止されたわけではないが、
預金金利は近年、市場金利の低下に伴って低下傾向にある。 
■ 市場化改革の成果を銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着させられるかが今後の焦点に 
PBOCは、2019年頃までに政策金利をリバースレポ7日物金利（短期）と MLF（中長期）の 2本立てと
する金融政策運営の枠組みを整え、新LPRの導入でその枠組みを明確化した（月岡（2024）参照）。
2024年7月には、政策金利をリバースレポ7日物に一本化し、LPRの参照金利もMLFからこれに移行さ
せた。中長期金利を市場の決定に委ねる形へと歩を進めたのである。 
PBOCは同時に、誘導目標とする短期金利を一定の範囲内で安定させるため金利コリドー（回廊）の
図表3 新LPRと銀行貸出金利 図表4 金利コリドー

仕組みも整える（図表4）。2024年7月、流動性を吸収するレポと供給するリバースレポの翌日物の臨
時オペを開始し、レポ金利をリバースレポ7日物の▲0.2％Pt、リバースレポ金利を7日物の＋0.5％Ptに
設定することでコリドーの幅を狭めた。さらに、2026年6月には臨時オペの金利をそれぞれ▲0.25％Pt、
＋0.25％Ptに調整し、誘導目標とするDR001が臨時オペ金利の上下限を継続的に逸脱した場合にオペを
実施すると明確化した。リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを実施する準備を整えた形である。 
このように、PBOCは政策金利による短期市場金利の誘導精度を高めるとともに、市場金利の変化を
実際の銀行貸出金利へと円滑に波及させるメカニズムの構築を進めてきた。今回、政策金利をリバー
スレポ翌日物金利に変更し、LPRを介さずにDR001を直接参照する貸出金利の決定方式を導入したこ
とは、一連の市場化改革における重要な節目と位置づけることができる。DR001参照型の金利は指標
金利としてのLPRを代替するものではなく、PBOCは両者を併存させながら金利波及経路を改善するア
プローチを続けるとみられる。今後は、市場化改革の成果を商業銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着さ
せられるかが焦点となろう。

は否定～」Mizuho RT EXPRESS（7月4日）

ネス・エクスプレス（10月26日）

## Document 042: 20261027【投研報告】市場快訊_MSCI降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待

MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」 直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market）」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於 2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至「邊境市場（Frontier Market
）」，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但 MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但 MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低

觸及 4,907.37 點。雖然 20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度下跌 1.22%
，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」，原則上將不再納入 MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外。相關改革有助改

### Page 3

不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續，以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證，我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至 2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異，區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響，以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

AI 資本支出侵蝕現金流，科技巨頭財報拖累科技股 
                                   2026 年 10 月 30 日 
一、 市場動態 
1. AI 資本支出與現金流疑慮再起，那斯達克領跌：美股 10 月 29 日主要指數收黑，S&P 
500 下跌 0.30%、Nasdaq 下跌 1.38%、道瓊下跌 0.25%、費半下跌 1.42%，費半 ETF
盤後跌幅進一步擴大至逾 3%。市場下跌主要因 Alphabet 財報營收與獲利低於預期、
搜尋廣告業務成長放緩，加上大幅增加資本支出導致單季自由現金流轉負，股價重挫
12.64%，再度引發市場對 AI 投資報酬的疑慮，賣壓亦擴及半導體及軟體類股。其他
盤後公布財報的科技公司表現亦出現雜音，Amazon 資本支出明顯高於預期，近 12
個月自由現金流轉為淨流出，Intel 則因 PC 需求疲弱下修財測，使科技股盤後賣壓進
一步擴大。不過，當日 S&P 500 約 81%成分股上漲，顯示市場賣壓主要集中於 AI 相
關大型科技股，而非整體市場同步走弱。 
2. Fed 按兵不動但兩票主張升息，12 月升息機率仍高：Fed 於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 維
持利率於 3.75%～4.00%，Cook 與 Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，期貨市場隱含 12 月
升息機率約 85%。9 月 PCE 年增 3.6%，10 年期美債殖利率在 5%附近震盪。 
3. 中東情勢緊張，油價維持百元以上高檔：布蘭特原油 10 月 28 日升至每桶 115 美元附
近，WTI 亦站上 102 美元。高油價增加通膨與 Fed 升息壓力，也推升 AI 資料中心電
力成本，持續壓抑科技股評價。

二、 大型科技企業財報重點： 
1. 雲端業務全面加速，AI 需求仍未降溫：本週公布財報的大型科技企業中，雲端業務成
長普遍加速。Alphabet 旗下 Google Cloud 營收 307.09 億美元、年增 103%，TPU
系統銷售成為重要動能；Microsoft Azure 年增 48%，下季財測進一步加速至 49～
50%；Amazon AWS 營收 459.8 億美元、年增 39.3%，為近五年最快增速。Intel 資
料中心與 AI 營收亦年增 78%，創 15 年來最強成長。各家均表示算力需求仍高於可用
供給，顯示 AI 基礎建設需求尚未出現降溫跡象。 
2. 資本支出持續攀升，自由現金流表現決定股價分歧：Microsoft 於 10 月 28 日盤後公
布 2027 會計年度第一季財報，營收 922.79 億美元、年增 18.8%，營業利益率達
49.8%，自由現金流亦維持正值，整體財報優於市場預期。相較之下，Alphabet 雲端
業務表現亮眼，但廣告業務成長未達預期，且單季資本支出達 513.55 億美元，導致
自由現金流轉為負 90.65 億美元，股價於 10 月 29 日重挫 12.64%；Amazon 單季資
本支出則達 773 億美元，近 12 個月自由現金流轉為淨流出 424 億美元，加上第四季
營收財測中值低於預期，盤後股價下跌逾 6%。資本支出指引方面，Alphabet 維持 2026
年 1,950 億至 2,050 億美元，Amazon 維持約 2,200 億美元，Microsoft 則因第三方

三、 市場看法：
1. 市場不再單純獎勵 AI 投資，開始要求可驗證的財務報酬：Microsoft 與 Alphabet 同

樣大舉投資 AI，股價反應卻明顯分歧，關鍵在於 AI 投資是否已轉化為獲利與現金流。
我們認為，市場評價標準已由「是否投資 AI」轉向「AI 投資能否帶來足夠的營收成長
與現金流」，具備穩定現金流與明確商業化能力的企業將相對受青睞，而自由現金流持
續為負、高度依賴外部融資的業者則可能面臨評價壓縮。

2. 雲端未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求尚未出現結構性反轉：Microsoft 商業 RPO 達 6,770
億美元、年增 73%，Oracle RPO 約 6,640 億美元，AWS 與 Google Cloud backlog

3. 資本支出 2027 年仍將成長，台灣 AI 供應鏈持續受惠：四大雲端／平台業者 2026 年

資本支出合計約 7,200～7,400 億美元，且 2027 年仍將增加。元大投顧認為，Google
擴大 TPU 對外銷售及 Microsoft 持續部署 GPU 與自研 Maia 加速器，將帶動伺服器
ODM、散熱及機殼需求，受惠供應鏈包括廣達、奇鋐、雙鴻、健策、富世達及聯發科。
融資面則宜留意 AI 相關企業公司債利差及再融資條件變化。
4. Fed 鷹派立場限制評價空間，短線震盪可能延續：12 月升息預期升溫、通膨與油價維
持高檔，使 10 年期美債殖利率停留在 5%附近，壓抑高評價科技股評價擴張。只要
AI 需求未出現結構性反轉，維持短線審慎，對科技股中期偏正向看法。

四、市場展望：
1. 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：本週財報顯示雲端營收加速
成長與未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求仍強；但資本支出攀升、自由現金流轉弱，加上 Fed
可能於 12 月升息，短線科技股波動恐將延續。中期而言，AI 產業將由全面成長轉向基本
面分化，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健且 AI 投資已能轉化為獲利的企業，如雲端與 AI 軟體
平台領先者，以及先進製程、伺服器、散熱等關鍵供應鏈，風險報酬相對有利；對高度依
賴外部融資、自由現金流持續為負的企業則宜維持審慎，並留意零組件成本上升對 PC 等
消費性電子需求的排擠效應。
2. 美債避險功能仍在，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：10 月 29 日科技股下跌時，10 年期

美債殖利率由約 5.05%回落至約 4.99%，顯示美債仍具一定避險效果。惟 Fed 鷹派立場
及油價高檔使短天期公債易受升息預期影響，超長天期公債則面臨財政赤字與期限溢價壓
力，中天期公債風險報酬相對均衡。投資級債中期維持偏正向；高收益債則須留意 AI 相
關發行人自由現金流轉負及利差擴大風險。

3. 下週財報焦點：Apple、Palantir 與 AMD 接棒，檢驗 AI 需求延續性：下週（11/2～11/6）

將關注 iPhone 新機需求、記憶體成本對毛利率的影響，以及 AI 軟體商業化進度；AMD
與 Arista Networks 將於 11 月 3 日盤後公布，AMD 將提供 AI 加速器（Instinct GPU）
與伺服器 CPU 需求的最新證據，Arista 則可觀察資料中心網路設備需求。在本週雲端業

者確認資本支出持續成長後，下週財報將進一步檢驗 AI 供應鏈訂單能否轉化為營收與獲
利（詳見圖 4）。

• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

### Page 1

ab 11 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Nan Ya Plastics
Glass fabric capacity expansion to strengthen
vertical integration
What's new
NYP announced plans to invest NT$11.5bn to add 102mn meters of glass fabric capacity
(+17% vs. existing 590mn meters capacity) in Zhangbin, Taiwan, by end-2029.
According to management, the expansion aims to meet growing demand from AI
servers, networking equipment and increasingly complex PCBs, with 60–70% of the

additional capacity allocated for internal use. Capacity will flexibly serve conventional E-
glass and AI-grade specialty glass fabric, with specialty products targeted to reach ~40%
of glass fabric revenue in 2-3 years. NYP is also expanding upstream yarn production and
adding over 500 weaving looms to support its planned glass fabric capacity expansion
by end-2029.
Implications
NYP: Glass fabric expansion to strengthen vertical integration; 1.5–2.0% EM OP

upside from 2030
We view the expansion positively, as glass fabric remains the most supply-constrained
product across NYP's CCL value chain, with the highest OPM of c40% in 3Q26 for glass
fabric. We expect further margin improvement in 4Q26, driven by continued price hikes
and a higher specialty glass fabric mix amid strong AI-related demand. Over the longer
term, the expansion should strengthen NYP's vertical integration and secure upstream
glass fabric supply for downstream CCL production. Assuming full utilization and

current ASP and margins, we estimate the 17% capacity expansion could lift EM OP by
1.5–2.0% from 2030 onwards, with further upside potential from specialty product mix
upgrades.
E-glass fabric: limited impact on external supply
NYP's planned capacity expansion of 102mn meters per year is equivalent to ~2% of
total industry capacity according to our estimates. We expect limited impact on external
E-glass supply, as NYP plans to use 60-70% of output internally, leaving only ~30-40mn

meters annually for external sales before accounting for specialty fabric allocation.
AI-grade specialty fabric: supply constraints likely to persist
We expect supply tightness to persist amid specialty yarn constraints and qualified fabric
production ramp-up. NYP's undisclosed capacity mix further limits visibility on
incremental AI-grade fabric supply.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and PT at NT$390/share
NYP is trading at 10x 27E P/E and 3.5x 27E P/B on our estimates.  Our 26/27E dividend

yields are 3.8%/6.3% at the current share price. We expect limited near-term valuation
impact from the glass fabric expansion, given its expected completion by end-2029.

Forecast returns

Market return assumption 6.4%
Forecast excess return 24.5%
Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

2026.10.30   
현대차 (005380)

26.3Q Review: 이제 올라가야 할 때 Company Brief

본업 Review: 26.3Q 매출액 45.1조원, 영업이익 2.6조원  
현대차는 26.3Q 매출액 45.1원 (-3.4% YoY, -8.3% QoQ), 영업이익 2.6조원 (+5.5% 
YoY, -6.1% QoQ, +5.9% OPM) 등의 실적을 시현했다 [iM증권 추정치 매출액: 
45.3조원, 영업이익: 2.4조원]. 매출액은 시장 컨센서스에 부합했고, 영업이익의 경우 
시장 컨센서스였던 2.4조원을 소폭 상회했다. 주요 구성 요소 별 내용은 아래와 같다.  
① P: 26.3Q HEV 판매 대수는 16.6만대를 기록하며 전사 판매 대비 비중 
18.2% 달성 (+18.8% YoY, -11.1% QoQ). 미국의 경우 국내 공장 생산 
감소에 따른 수출 부진으로 도매 판매는 소폭 감소했으나, 판매 비중은 
25.2%로 전반적인 믹스는 직전 분기와 유사. 26.3Q 평균환율이 직전 분기 
대비 크게 하락했으나, 전년 동기 기저 탓에 오히려 + 환율 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매 대수는 91.8만대로 전년 동기 대비 11.5% 감소 (이하 
YoY 기준, 미국: -10.0%, 유럽: -18.1% 국내: -27.2% 인도: +18.3%). 
26.2Q 안전 공업 화재에 이어 26.3Q에 발생한 파업의 영향으로 생산 
대수는 감소. 국내 공장에서의 생산 차질은 내수 판매에 더해 수출 감소, 즉 
전체적인 판매 축소로 연결됨. 26.4Q부터 생산 관련 노이즈는 발생하지 
않을 전망. 이에 더해 아반떼, 투싼 등의 신차 판매 효과를 통한 반등 가능.  
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 직전 분기와 유사한 수준으로 발생한 것으로 파악 
(25.2Q 0.8조원, 25.3Q 1.8조원, 25.4Q 1.5조원, 26.1Q 0.8조원, 26.2Q 
0.9조원). 이란 사태에 따른 주요 원자재 가격 상승 압력은 계속 존재하나, 
내부 원가 절감 정책으로 약 5,000억원의 원가 상승분을 상쇄. 현대차는 
26.4Q 원가 절감 정책의 효과가 더 크게 나타날 것이라고 언급. 26.3Q 
기말 환율이 전년 동기 대비 급락함에 따라 판매보층충당부채전입액 또한 
감소 (26.3Q 매출원가, 판매관리비 각 전년 동기 대비 1.7%, 19.2% 감소). 
금융 부문의 경우 26.3Q 매출액 9.1조원 (+27.8% YoY), 영업이익 4,710억원 (-
18.2% YoY, -37.9%) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 완성차 판매가 전반적으로 
위축되었음에도 외형 성장을 이어갔으나, 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 등 전반적인 
조달 관련 비용이 증가했다. 일부 EV를 중심으로 중고차 가격이 소폭 하락한 점 또한 
HCA 등 주요 부문 이익 감소의 배경이다. 26.3Q 현대차 순이익은 1,932억원을 
기록했는데, 이는 영업외손익이 전년 동기 789억원에서 229억원으로 감소했기 
때문이다. 현대차는 주요 환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실 증가의 영향이라고 밝혔다. 
1) 생산 단에서의 노이즈가 연속해 발생하며 내수 판매 및 수출 감소가 불가피했으나, 
2) 원자재 가격 상승 영향을 내부 원가 절감 정책을 통해 상쇄했고, 3) 주요 
평균환율이 하락했으나 기말환율 또한 급락하며 판매보증충당부채전입액 감소 효과가 
나타나 시장 기대를 소폭 상회하는 수익성이 관찰되었다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
현대차는 2026년 도매 판매 가이던스를 416만대에서 400만대로 하향 조정했다. 
26.3Q 누적 도매 판매 대수는 288만대이기에 하향 조정은 불가피했다. 단 신규 
가이던스에 따르면 26.4Q 판매 목표는 약 110만대 수준이라는 것인데, 아반떼, 투싼, 
주요 모델 페이스리프트, GV80 HEV 등의 신차 효과가 얼마나 나타날지가 관건일 
것이다 (이하 분기 도매 판매, 25.1Q 100.1만대, 25.2Q 105.7만대, 25.3Q 103.8만대).

현대차 (005380)
2
미래 사업 업데이트: 자율주행은 계획대로, Atlas는 조금씩 변신중
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 특별히 SDV~Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트는 확인되지
않았으나 여러 매체를 통해 파악된 현대차그룹의 미래 사업 진행 상황은 다음과 같다.
① SDV/자율주행: 데이터 플라이 휠에 대한 중요성 강조. 27년 말부터 광주
권역 자율주행 실증 사업에 투입되는 차량은 Atria AI 활용. SDV 플랫폼이
적용된 차량 양산 시작은 29년. 양산 SDV 출하량 증가함에 따라 29년부터
GPU 5만개 이상으로 구성되는 새만금 데이터센터를 자체 운영을 목표함.
박민우 포티투닷 사장 겸 AVP 본부장이 부임한 이후 Tesla, nVIDIA 출신의
개발 인력을 충원 중인 상황. 26.05 그랜저 페이스리프트 모델에 플레오스
탑재 시작. 26.09부터 판매되고 있는 신형 아반떼에도 플레오스 탑재 완료.

② Boston Dynamics: 10.8 Rohit Prasad를 CEO로 선임. Rohit Prasad은
아마존에서 Alex 개발에 중추적인 역할을 수행했던 인물. 현대차그룹에서
직접 하드웨어 개발 및 양산을 챙기고, 소프트웨어에 역량을 높이겠다는
행보로 판단. 26.10 Atlas의 신규 덱스트러스를 공개했는데, 기존 제품 대비
자유도가 크게 증가 (7DoF→14DoF). 순수 직구동 방식을 여전히
고수함.10.27 Boston Dynamics가 주요 부품 업체에게 Atlas 1만대 물량의
부품 PO를 곧 발송할 것이라는 기사가 보도됨. 28년까지 연 3만대 CAPA의
공장 완공이라는 기존 계획 대비 양산 및 개발 속도가 빨라진다는 시그널임.
현대차그룹의 미래 사업 업데이트를 또 찾아볼 수 있을 가장 빠른 이벤트는 CES
2027이다. 다만 CES 2027에는 현대차가 아닌 그룹사가 참여함에 따라 해당 행사에서

유의미한 업데이트 내용은 찾아보기 힘들 것이며 이에 중장기 관점의 접근이 필요하다.
시장의 우려가 반영된 주가 하락: 빨라질 속도와 올라올 본업에 주목
현대차 주가는 26.2H 접어들며 크게 하락하고 있고, CES 2026 전후의 상승폭을 사실상
모두 반납했다 (연초 기준 -1.3%, 26.2H 이후 -41.7%). 또한 최근 시장 반등
흐름에서도 사실상 소외되고 있다. 현대차를 바라보는 투자자들의 우려는 아래와 같다.
첫째, CID 전후로 Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트가 너무 부족하다는 것이다. 26.08
현대차 CID 행사를 앞두고 많은 투자자들은 빅테크 업체들의 지분 투자 및 Atlas
개발과 관련해 새로운 내용을 기대한 것이 사실이다. 이런 상황에서 유럽, 내수 등 주요
지역 에서의 판매 업황 또한 좋지 않다 보니, 시장의 실망은 더욱 컸다. 전술한 것과
같이 CES 2027에 현대차가 참여하지 않는다는 점에서 내년에 대한 기대도 크지 않다.

둘째, 내수와 유럽 업황이 그룹사 대비 너무 좋지 못하다는 점이다. 26.3Q 현대차 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 27.2%, 18.1% 감소했다. 반면 26.3Q 기아 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 2.2%, 18.7% 증가했다. 특히 유럽에서의 부진은
EV 라인업이 상대적으로 빈약하고, 중국 업체와의 경쟁이 심화되고 있다는 구조적
문제에서 출발하기에, 투자자들은 26년을 넘어 27, 28년의 성장까지 우려하고 있다
단 우리는 Boston Dynamics의 Atlas는 양산이라는 방향성이 설정된지 오래되지
않았다는 점을 기억해야 한다. 초기 단계부터 양산을 위해 개발된 Tesla Optimus 대비
개발 속도가 빠를 수는 없다. 1~2만대 수준의 데모 제품이 확보되고, 이를 바탕으로
데이터 수집이 일정 부분 확보되는 이후부터 관련 사업 확대 속도는 빨라 질것이다.

취약 지역 판매 업황의 경우에도 내수를 시작으로 점진적으로 개선될 것이다. 생산 차질
외에도 Tesla 및 중국 OEM들의 시장 침투가 우려됨은 분명하지만, 주력 차종 중에서도
국내 판매 볼륨이 큰 아반떼와 투싼의 신차 효과는 분명 기대되는 지점이다. 유럽에서의
반등은 시간이 필요할 것이나, 일단 아이오닉 3가 26.4Q부터 본격적으로 판매될
예정이다. 단 이는 큰 변동 없이 IAA가 2028년부터 시행된다는 전제에 근거하고 있다.
현대차 목표주가 600,000원 유지: 여전한 Multiple 할증 요인
현대차 목표주가를 600,000원으로 유지한다. 26년 EPS 기준 6~8배 수준의 P/E
Multiple을 부여받고 있는 Peer Group들은 중국 시장 M/S 축소로 수익성이 급락하고
있고, 미래 사업 경쟁력은 여전히 전무하다. 현대차의 Multiple 할증 요인은 충분하다.

그림16. IAA 법안 개요

자료: iM증권 리서치본부

그림17. 유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 추이 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

IAA는 EU판 IRA로 볼 수 있음. 현대차그룹의 경우 현대차 (터키, 체코)와 기아 (슬로바키아) 모두 유렵 현지
생산 라인을 이미 확보했고, 현대모비스를 중심으로 한 부품 자급률 비중 이미 높음. 또한 주요 부품에 있어
FTA 체결국의 원산지를 EU산과 동일하게 적용하는 규정 존재. 실제 발효까지 큰 변수가 없다면 국내 완성차
업체의 경우 구조적으로 수혜 클 것. 단 중국 업체들에 대한 명시적 배제 규정이 없다는 점은 한계 중 하나. 
IAA 주요 조항 주요 내용
적용 대상
공공조달 차량, 공적 지원(구매 보조금 등)을 받는 차량, 법인차가 대상임. 전기
차, PHEV, 수소차에 적용됨. 2028년부터 회원국은 무공해 및 저공해이면서 EU
산인 차량이 아니면 법인차를 지원할 수 없음. 판매 금지가 아니라 수요 측 혜택
을 역내 생산과 연동하는 구조
역내 부품 요건
1단계(발효 6개월 후)는 EU 조립, 배터리 제외 부품가치 70% EU산, 셀 포함
배터리 핵심부품 3개 EU산을 요구함. 2단계(1단계 3년 후)는 배터리 핵심부품
5개(셀, 양극재,BMS 포함)와 전동 파워트레인·전장 가치 50% EU산이 추가됨.
소형차 특례
전장 4.2m 이하 전기 승용차가 대상임. EU 조립에 더해 부품가치 70%와
배터리 핵심부품 3개 중 하나만 충족하면 됨. 충족 시 CO2 규제상 1.3대로 인정
됨(2025~34년).
FDI 조건 1억 유로 초과 투자가 대상이며, 지분 49% 상한, EU 인력 50% 이상, EU산 투 입재 30% 이상 등의 조건이 붙음.
FTA·관세동맹국 예외
FTA나 관세동맹을 맺은 국가의 부품은 EU산과 동등한 부품으로 인정되며,
정부조달협정(GPA) 가입국도 일부 조항에 포함됨. 단 최종 조립국까지 인정되
는지는 불명확하며, 조문상으로는 EU, EEA 역내 조립만 인정되는 것으로 해석
됨. 따라서 한국산 부품은 EU산으로 인정 가능하나, 한국이나 튀르키예에서 조
립한 완성차는 요건 미충족 가능성이 높음.
FTA 예외 배제 조건
집행위는 위임법으로 특정국을 동등 대상에서 제외할 수 있음. 해당국이 EU 기업
에 내국민 대우를 하지 않는 경우, 공급망 의존도나 공급 안보 위협이 있는 경우,
해당 협정의 예외 조항에 해당하는 경우가 배제 사유임. 중국은 FTA가 없어 애초
에 대상이 아님.
2020 2021 2022 2023
대당 생산원가 30,683 32,029 35,079 38,140
대당 판매가격 34,404 27,557 31,244 33,560
1인당 인건비 70,981 74,904 78,348 88,198
통상적으로 중국 OEM의 생산 원가 (중국 생산 가정)은 글로벌 OEM 대비 30% 낮다고 추정됨. BYD의 경우 돌핀 등과 같
은 C 세그먼트 차량에서의 유럽 수익성이 낮지만, A~B 세그먼트 급의 돌핀 서프의 경우 매우 높은 수익성 시현. 이는 유럽
시장 내의 A~B 세그먼트 경쟁 강도가 낮다는 점에서 기인. 즉 100% 수준의 관세가 부과되지 않는다면 BYD의 가격 경쟁
력은 유지되는 것. 따라서 EU는 2,000유로 수준의 중국 OEM의 대당 이익을 상쇄하기 위해 이에 준하는 수준으로 지급되
는 EV 구매 보조금 및 법인 차량 세제혜택을 검토 중인 것이고, 이를 IAA에 담아낼지가 핵심.
유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 및 판매가격 추이

그림23. 26.3Q 국내 완성차 실적 Summary

자료: iM증권 리서치본부

현대차 미주: 펠리세이드 리콜 이슈 존재하나, 메타플랜트 및 기존 앨러배마 공
장 HEV 생산을 통한 시장 진출 속도 빨라질 것으로 전망
인도: GM 탈레가온 공장 인수를 통한 생산량 확대에 나설 예정이고, 이
를 통한 인도 내수 수요 대응 및 글로벌 신흥 시장 진출 또한 목표
유럽: 기아 대비 EV 라인업의 폭에 열위에 있으나, 캐스퍼 EV, EV 3를
통해 EV 수요 회복 국면을 앞두고 있는 유럽 시장 대응에 나설 예정
기아 미주: 텔룰라이드 HEV 출시를 통해 Mid-Size  HEV 수요 대응 이뤄지
고 있음. 스포티지 및 셀토스 등 HEV를 통한 기존 차급 대응 강화 전망
인도: 2030년까지 10개 차종 출시 목표하고 있고, 이를 통해 2030년
41만대 도매 판매, 시장 점유율 7.6% 달성 가이던스를 제시함
유럽: EV2 출시를 통한 EV 라인업 완성도 높임. 소형 해치백 차량과 신
규 HEV 차량 출시, 그리고 PBV 판매 본격화를 통한 초과 성장 목표

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 222: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │3 
26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

## Document 267: An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-13).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
An RBA peak of greater conviction
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:56 November 13 2026 Share
We already believed the RBA cash rate was likely at a peak, even if RBA officials were less
convinced. A deeper housing downturn and early signs of cracks in the consumer sector
point to an even softer 2027. Fading inflation risks from oil also boost our conviction on
rates being at a peak.

### Page 2

It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking the cash rate after the November
increase. Since then, our conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we will publish
the November Market Outlook with revised forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing
market have all been revised down in 2027. Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for calendar 2027 more decisively inside the

RBA’s 2–3% target range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment rate forecast has
been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash
rate from here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.
A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The housing market has undershot our
forecasts over the past couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been coming in
under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan inquiries have taken another leg down in October after

the September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior months, with the additional impact
of the November rate hike still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer. Consumer income and spending have
so far been quite resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest rates. But our Westpac
Card Tracker is starting to show that discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite weak. We would not over-emphasise

this point, but it does imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB) meeting, business survey data
has shown weaker conditions and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show further
evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-labour cost shock that is not being fully
passed through to consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil and fuel prices are already materially

lower than the track assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-fourth lower than
on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of fuel costs to other prices, as well as
easing consumer inflation perceptions and expectations.
In the media conference following the November meeting, Governor Bullock flagged that it was a

good time to “take stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the market read this as a
signal that the MPB would pause at the December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to downside risks than the internal
members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is looking even less likely given signs
in the data and surveys. The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and senior

officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though, a rate hike in 2027 would require
either a material new inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would constitute a policy
error on the part of the RBA (something that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the
talents and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with the dissenting voters in the MPB

that the downside risks need more attention. Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to February, we think there will be more
evidence that the domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation outlook with it.

### Page 3

## Document 337: First Impressions_ NZ retail card spending, October 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ retail card spending, October 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#retail #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ retail card spending, October 2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:26 November 12 2026 Share
Card spending was surprisingly weak again in October.
Retail spending
Monthly change: -0.7% (prev: -0.5%, Westpac forecast: +1.4%)
Annual change: +0.3% (prev: +0.9%)

### Page 2

Core retail spending (excluding fuel/autos) 
Monthly change: -1.2% (prev: -0.7%) 
Annual change: -0.8% (prev: +0.1%)

Retail spending on credit and debit cards was reportedly down 0.7% in October, a third straight monthly
decline. Spending was down across all categories with the exception of fuel, which saw a 4.8% rise in
spending – and with fuel prices around 10% higher over the month, that still points to a decline in
spending volumes. 
We should note that we have had concerns for some time about the coverage of this data. The monthly
result was again significantly weaker than what we recorded for spending on Westpac-issued credit
and debit cards (ANZ also reported an increase in spending on its own figures). And as a lead indicator
of the quarterly retail survey (which feeds into GDP), it has undershot in every one of the last six
quarters. We don’t suggest that spending is growing strongly by any means – and it is likely the case
that higher fuel prices are crowding out spending elsewhere. But we suspect that the true picture is not
as weak as today’s release suggests. 
A drop in oil prices since the start of November has provided some relief for fuel prices at the pump,
although they remain high. We anticipate that growth in consumer spending volumes will remain
subdued in the near term, with regional variations dependent on exposure to the well-performing export
sector. What happens beyond this year will depend on how the Middle East conflict evolves and how
the RBNZ responds to the rebound in inflation.

黎光岱 
David.li@ctbcsis.com

M31 (6643 TT)

維持評等 
增加持股(Overweight) 
隨權利金營收比重提升，未來營運狀況更趨穩定 
⚫ 3Q26營收增幅低於預期 
⚫ 3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數 
⚫ 提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均 
3Q26營收增幅低於預期：M31 3Q26營收為 4.95億元(QoQ+17.4%，
YoY+39.6%)，以美金計價營收為 1,546 萬美元 (QoQ+16.2%，
YoY+31.0%)，營收成長受惠於客戶開案動能強勁、權利金穩健成長，
以及北美高價值專案營收認列。營業費用為 4.8 億元(QoQ+11.2%，
YoY+21.1%)，因調整部分獎金發放時點而使本季營業費用較高。營業
利益為 730萬元，相較 2Q26由虧轉盈。EPS為 0.35元。 
3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數：3Q26授權金收入為 4.06億
元(QoQ+17%，YoY+47%)，約佔營收 82%，創歷史單季第三高。Fabless
授權金收入以美元計算 QoQ+3%，YoY+127%，主要由北美客戶先進
製程專案及晶圓代工平台合作案帶動，其中包含公司首個 2奈米以下
的 GAA架構專案，Fabless客戶持續提升對 16奈米以下先進製程技術
的採用比例，先進製程專案已成為授權業務成長的主要驅動力。3Q26
權利金收入為 0.89億元(QoQ+19%，YoY+14%)，約佔營收 18%，創
1Q23以來新高，為近 15季最佳表現，Foundry權利金收入持續成長，
反映既有平台專案逐步放量並持續貢獻營收，未來 A14、2奈米及 CSP
等新案機會可望進一步延續公司於先進製程領域的成長動能。 
提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均：公司將於 4Q26持續深
化與北美 IDM客戶客製化記憶體平台、美系晶圓廠 12奈米平台的合
作，完成與大型晶圓廠 28奈米 Foundation IP合約簽署，2奈米以下
與多項先進製程專案持續開展，將帶動授權金維持高檔。公司目標
2026年 EPS轉正，且全年營收較 2025年成長。2027年授權金目標成
長約 10%，權利金挑戰 30%以上成長，隨著更多客戶專案逐步進入大
量生產階段，預期先進製程權利金收入將進入加速成長期，16奈米以
下先進製程權利金比重將提升至 50%。預期 2027年整體營收分布更
為平穩，目標上下半年分別為 45:55。 
我們基於 2027年預估 EPS 6.35元*60倍 PER，調整目標價至 381元，
維持增加持股評等。風險因子:專案進程與製程平台導入進度。

## Document 375: NZ labour market review chartpack, Q3 2026 (2026-11-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ labour market review chartpack, Q3 2026 (2026-11-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

LABOUR MARKET CHARTPACK
4 Nov 2026

### Page 2

Westpac New Zealand Economics
2
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Key points – September quarter 2026
• The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in labour market conditions. The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an
almost five-year run of increases. The unemployment rate is now at its highest level since September 2015.
• There were mixed messages in terms of jobs growth: the Household Labour Force Survey reported a 0.1% fall in employment, while the employer-

focused Quarterly Employment Survey suggested a modest rise. The Monthly Employment Indicator had also pointed to a small rise in jobs.
• The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in the participation rate. These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly large
increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error in the survey rather than a
genuine surge.
• We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message here is one of softness. Our

best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population.
• While the economy has returned to growth over the last year or so, hiring activity remains weak. This may be an ongoing legacy of employment trends in
previous years, when firms were relatively reluctant to lay people off during the slowdown, which then potentially left them overstaffed even as activity
started to pick up again. It’s not clear how long this effect can persist, though.

• Wage growth remains muted. Although cost-of-living pressures have increased on workers, the existing degree of spare capacity in the labour market
doesn’t give them much leverage to negotiate larger pay increases.
• Overall, we judge these results to be weaker than the RBNZ expected, in particular the rise in the unemployment rate. This data supports the views of
those on the policy committee who were less convinced in October about the potential for second-round inflation pressures in the economy.

### Page 3

Westpac New Zealand Economics
3
Employment
• The number of people employed fell by 0.1% in the September quarter, according to the
Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS). Previous quarters were revised down a little,
reflecting downward revisions to the working-age population estimates. Annual employment
growth slowed from 0.9% to 0.7%.
• This was substantially softer than our forecast of a 0.5% rise for the quarter. Our forecast
was based on the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI), a comprehensive measure that is

drawn from income tax data. While there are some differences in coverage for these two
measures, any significant divergence between the two is most likely to reflect sampling error
in the HLFS.
• Meanwhile, the employer-focused Quarterly Employment Survey (QES) recorded a 0.1% rise
in the number of filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. (This survey is of course vulnerable
to sampling error as well.)
• We’re most inclined towards the MEI measure of employment, given the breadth of its

coverage. But our best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not
yet fast enough to absorb the growth in the adult population – hence the further rise in the
unemployment rate.

Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us

### Page 4

Westpac New Zealand Economics
4
Labour force participation
• The surprising fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a drop in the participation rate
from 70.6% to 70.4%.
• These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were suggestive of sampling error in the
survey. Any such errors largely wash out when it comes to the unemployment rate, however,
which is why we recommend focusing on this measure.

• Setting aside the usual quarterly volatility, labour force participation remains caught
between two competing forces. One is cyclical weakness, especially among younger people,
who are finding it difficult to break into a “low-fire, low-hire” labour market. The teen
unemployment rate of around 25% is a record high going back to the start of the survey
in 1986.
• The other force is rising participation among the over-60s (who also make up a growing

share of the population). Kiwis have had a growing propensity to work beyond the NZ Super
age, as there is no penalty for doing so. It’s unclear to what extent this can be considered
an increase in the pool of available workers: older workers tend to have low mobility
between jobs, and if they find themselves out of work are more likely to leave the labour
force altogether.

### Page 5

Westpac New Zealand Economics
5
Unemployment and underutilisation
• The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7% in the September quarter. This was the
highest rate since September 2015.
• While there are other indicators of spare capacity in the labour market, we’re not convinced
that there is additional information to draw from them this time.
• The broader underutilisation measure, which includes discouraged jobseekers and part-
time workers wanting more hours, was steady at 13.8% after having risen sharply from

12.9% last quarter. This measure is both higher (by definition) and more variable than the
unemployment rate, but tells a similar story in that it’s around an 11-year high.
• There was a marked 1.6% fall in the number of hours worked (although ‘usual’ hours were
down just 0.4%). However, we suspect this was largely driven by Matariki – a floating holiday
that won’t be captured in the seasonal adjustment.
• This may also account for the 2.5% rise in part-time employment, against a 0.7% fall in

full-time employment. Some workers may have been pushed into the part-time category
(less than 30 hours a week) simply by virtue of only working four days during the
survey week.

### Page 6

Westpac New Zealand Economics
6
Hiring
• Business confidence surveys have shown a lift in hiring intentions in recent months, as firms
have shaken off the initial shock of the Iran conflict and the surge in fuel prices.
• However, those stated intentions aren’t obviously turning into reality yet. Job advertisements
were down about 1% for the September quarter in seasonally adjusted terms, and they
remain well below pre-Covid levels. Job ads will capture ‘churn’ within existing roles as well

as new job creation, but both of these would be associated with a strong labour market.
• With the working-age population growing at the same time, the result was a modest fall in
the vacancy rate, one of the RBNZ’s highest-ranked measures of labour market tightness.
• We estimate that there are around 1.9 people unemployed for every job vacancy, up from
around 1.8 people this time last year.
• Firms have told us that they were reluctant to lay off workers during the economic downturn

in 2023-24, as they were wary of the extreme labour shortages that had developed in the
previous boom years. As a result, they may find themselves still overstaffed even as demand
has started to pick up again, and haven’t needed to return to hiring yet.

### Page 7

Westpac New Zealand Economics
7
Population and migration
• The working-age population grew by around 1.0% over the last year, compared to a peak of
3.3% growth over 2023. Jobs growth (based on the less volatile MEI measure) is still lagging
behind population growth on an annual basis.
• The pool of potential workers is generally becoming older, with the Baby Boomers
increasingly reaching pension age and beyond, and with migrant arrivals tending to be an

older cohort than we saw pre-Covid – more in their 30s and 40s than their 20s.
• Net migration flows have started to pick up from their lows since late 2025. Departures of
New Zealanders have come down from their highs, though they remain above pre-Covid
levels, as the relative appeal of the Australian jobs market has narrowed.
• Foreign arrivals have picked up to some degree, although the growth appears to have been
mostly driven by students rather than those on work visas.

### Page 8

Westpac New Zealand Economics
8
Sectoral and regional trends
• Jobs growth has been relatively strong in government-connected sectors such as healthcare
and education, as well as in local and central government administration.
• Mining and utilities have seen strong growth in percentage terms, but neither of these
sectors are big employers in absolute terms.
• The construction sector is now starting to see a lift in employment, after a steep decline

over 2024-25. Residential building consents have risen strongly over the last year or so,
pointing to a growing pipeline of work for the homebuilding industry.
• Sectors that are connected to international tourism, such as transport and recreational
services, had previously seen strong employment growth, but this appears to have flattened
out in recent months.
• Employment trends have generally been more positive in the South Island than the North,

which likely reflects the greater influence of the agricultural sector – while not a large
employer in itself, farmers have benefited from high export prices and their spending has
flowed into other sectors.

### Page 9

Westpac New Zealand Economics
9
Wages
• The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter. Annual growth
remained around the lowest since 2021.
• While the overall result was in line with our forecast, the details were a little weaker than
we expected, with less contribution from private sector wages (up 0.5%) and a surprisingly
large 0.7% increase in the public sector.
• The distribution of pay rates has returned to pre-Covid trends. Only 44% of roles saw a

pay rise over the last year; of those roles that did see an increase, the average size has
converged on the 2-3% range.
• The LCI is not a measure of what workers actually receive – it reflects the cost to businesses
for a given unit of labour – and hence shouldn’t be compared to the inflation rate. A more
suitable measure is the analytical unadjusted LCI, which includes pay rises that are related
to productivity gains. This measure slowed from 3.1% to 2.9% on an annual basis, the lowest

since March 2021.
• There was a marked pickup in the proportion of firms reporting cost-of-living adjustments as
the main reason for pay increases. However, the size of those increases varied greatly, with
many of them below the rate of CPI inflation.
• This seems to reflect a shift in the justification for pay rises, rather than an overall increase in
wage pressures – firms are more likely to cite cost-of-living adjustments because they’re less
likely to cite demand-side pressures.

### Page 10

Westpac New Zealand Economics
10
International comparisons
• New Zealand’s unemployment rate is the 18th lowest in the OECD (out of 38 countries). This
ranking has been broadly unchanged over the last year or so.
• Notably, New Zealand’s unemployment rate is substantially above Australia’s, whereas
historically it has been lower on average. This shift in relative job opportunities has been a
key attractor for New Zealanders over the last couple of years – as it was in the early 2010s

when the Australian economy was benefiting from a boom in mining construction.
• However, that gap between unemployment rates is no longer widening, with the Australian
jobs market starting to soften this year as the RBA has raised interest rates to rein in inflation
(which was already running too high before the Iran conflict and the surge in oil prices). We
expect Australia’s unemployment to rise further over the year ahead.
• New Zealand has the 7th highest employment rate in the OECD (down from 6th last quarter).

We tend to have high rates of labour force participation for 15-64 year olds, and particularly
high participation for the over-65s, as the lack of means testing for NZ Superannuation
means there is no financial disincentive to working beyond pension age.

## Document 429: US MARKETS CALL_ S&P 500 Valuation Dives As Earnings Soar

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKETS CALL: S&P 500 Valuation Dives As Earnings Soar
Date:    5 hours ago
US MARKETS CALL: S&P 500 Valuation Dives As Earnings Soar

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKETS CALL: S&P 500
Valuation Dives As Earnings
Soar
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 7 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Baptiste Azais / Unsplash
The stock market dived last week. Investors priced in political backlash
against data centers after Democrats swept Congress. We anticipated
buying opportunities after the midterm elections, and Thursday’s selloff
delivered some. This remains an earnings-led bull market. Valuations are
falling while earnings are soaring. We still view the AI backlash as another

### Page 3

speed bump, not a roadblock. We are sticking with our 7,900 year-end
target for the S&P 500.
I. Performance
The Nasdaq entered correction territory on Thursday and closed the week
below its 200-day moving average (chart). It has done this before recently.
It rebounded sharply after its Spring 2025 "Liberation Day" plunge and
again after the Iran war started earlier this year, quickly resuming its
uptrend both times.
The equal-weight S&P 500 is holding up remarkably well. It remains near

its recent record high and above both its 50-day and 200-day moving
averages (chart). The damage has been concentrated in the AI trade, not
the broader market.

### Page 4

The Magnificent-7 are underperforming again. The MAGS ETF is up only
2.4% ytd, versus 7.8% for the S&P 500 (chart). The Impressive-493 also
took a hit this week. The XMAGS ETF includes Micron and the other
semiconductor stocks hit hardest in the selloff. It is still up 10.0% ytd.
Dow Theory remains bullish. The Dow Jones Industrial Average is holding
up well near its highs (chart). The Transports have lagged. They should
improve if oil prices continue to fall.

II. Earnings
Earnings season picked up this week. The blended Q3 S&P 500 EPS
growth estimate (i.e., actual results for companies that have reported,
estimates for those that haven’t yet) rose to 25.9% y/y from 23.8% a week
earlier, helped by AMD’s solid results. Q4’s estimate edged down to 29.7%

### Page 5

(chart). As we noted previously, Q2’s 50.4% y/y EPS growth rate was
boosted by mark-to-market gains at Alphabet and Amazon. Excluding
those, growth was closer to 25%, which is still very strong.
Forward earnings rose to a record $418.25. It is converging on the 2027
estimate of $426.28, which continues to climb (chart). Analysts’ 2028
consensus S&P 500 EPS estimate rose to $495.14; it’s been closing in on
$500—which is our target for 2030!  We are bullish, but not as bullish as

the analysts. The drop in the forward P/E this year suggests that investors
also believe analysts are too bullish on earnings.
Forward earnings for the S&P 500, S&P 400, and S&P 600 all rose to
record highs again last week (chart). Earnings strength remains broad.

### Page 6

S&P 500 forward revenues per share is up 15.9% y/y, while forward
earnings per share is up 39.0% y/y (chart).
III. Valuation
The S&P 500 forward P/E fell to 17.6 last week, below the 2025 Liberation
Day low (chart). P/E plunges typically accompany corrections and
recessions. The economy shows no sign of a recession anytime soon.
Investors instead fear irrational exuberance in earnings forecasts.

### Page 7

Since the bull market began on October 12, 2022, the S&P 500 has risen
106.2%. Forward earnings is up 75.5%, while the forward P/E is up 22.5%
(chart). The P/E and E both drove the rally until the forward P/E peaked in
October last year. Since then, E has done all the work. Investors are
finding it harder to predict the return on all the AI capital spending. They
won’t pay an excessive multiple given that uncertainty.
The S&P 500 Semiconductors industry accounts for much of the P/E drop.

The industry index's forward P/E has plunged to 13.1, well below the S&P
500’s (chart). Semis are now cheaper than they were during the 2022
bear market.

### Page 8

IV. Fixed Income
The futures market expects about two rate hikes over the next 12 months.
That explains why the 2-year Treasury yield remains well above the 3.88%
federal funds rate, even after easing to 4.46% following the midterms
(chart).
The 10-year Treasury yield continues to fall. It dropped to 4.84% and is
testing its uptrend line from last year’s low (chart). We’ll see if it bounces.
Either way, it is back in the 4.00%-5.00% “old normal” range where we feel

comfortable.

### Page 9

The 10-year TIPS yield fell to 2.62%. The yield spread between the 10-
year Treasury and TIPS, a widely used proxy for expected inflation, eased
to 2.22% (chart). That proxy tends to move with oil prices. The recent drop
in crude showed up here.
V. Commodities
The price of Brent crude oil fell to $98.32 a barrel in recent days (chart).
No news of the re-escalation of the war in the Middle East has emerged
since the midterms. Hopes for a US-Iran deal are reflected in the oil

market. We still see a risk of re-escalation.

### Page 10

Nearby gasoline futures dropped to $2.78 a gallon last week (chart). If
sustained, that should start to show up as lower inflation in a couple of
months. Renewed fighting in the Middle East would reverse that.
Steel prices show no sign of a slowdown in data center construction. US
Midwest hot-rolled coil rose to $1,357 a ton, the highest since 2022
(chart).

### Page 11

Agricultural prices have eased. The S&P GSCI Agriculture Spot index fell
to 376.77 last week (chart). Fears of a food price spike should subside.
💡

log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

### Page 12

## Document 442: What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-10-11
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it?
A closer look at the impact of China’s currency on China’s export growth.
Sean Yong/Reuters
UPDATED
October 11, 2026 12:00 a.m.
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
There is an argument—made in the recent Geneva
Report and by various former IMF officials—that
China’s current export surge is the simple
consequence of the housing downturn and the
resulting weakness in Chinese internal demand.*
The weakness in the RMB in this story is entirely
incidental to the increase in exports, which is, more or
less, a mechanical response to weak internal demand.

Cannot sell excavators at home? Export them.
Built more modern car capacity than your internal
market supports? Export that too…
The fall in the nominal RMB over the course of 2022
and the first half of 2023 in nominal and read terms
was simply a function of domestic macroeconomic
weakness, and a growing rate differential. It wasn’t an
active policy choice by China’s central bank.

### Page 3

3
Now, some might note that China’s currency doesn’t
always move with rate differentials. The RMB didn’t
consistently get pulled up when Chinese rates were
higher than U.S. rates (2002-2020 generally
speaking), and while it did fall when rate differentials
moved against the RMB in 2022 and 2023, it then
stabilized and has even appreciated modestly since
the middle of last year. That appreciation hasn’t been
big enough to correct the deep undervaluation of the

RMB, but it came at a time when the PBOC policy
rate (the reverse repo rate) was well below the U.S.
policy rate.
China doesn’t have an open financial account. As a
result, equilibrium in the foreign exchange market is
thus driven far more by exporter conversion and
current account flows than by rate differentials and
financial flows.** That, together with China’s ongoing
management of the exchange rate and a trade surplus
that can generate significant inflows, means that the

### Page 4

4
value of the RMB is much more of a policy variable
under the independent control of the Chinese
authorities than a simple byproduct of monetary and
fiscal policies.
The modern IMF is reluctant to accept this
conclusion. Its surveillance has generally ignored
China’s currency management (even if that
management is relatively obvious; see the data on FX
settlement) and its recent comprehensive assessment
of China’s economy completely ignored the balance of

payments and exchange rate management.
But it is pretty obvious to the market, even if the
PBOC, rather absurdly, insists that “market forces
play a decisive role in determining the RMB
exchange rate.”
I doubt the PBOC can win this argument. I agree
with Mark Sobel:
“The renminbi is still heavily managed and the
authorities have acted to slow appreciation. The paper
states that the PBoC phased out ‘regular’ foreign
exchange intervention in 2017. But it has many

means at its disposal to achieve the same effect
through extensive capital controls, administrative
guidance, signalling at the fix and deploying state-
owned commercial banks. These means are heavily
used. Thus, the word ‘regular’ must be taken with a
pinch of salt. The comparison to Japan, which

### Page 5

5
intervenes occasionally, is spurious – the yen largely
floats freely in integrated global markets. The same
cannot be said for the renminbi.”
In reality, the PBOC’s daily fix now plays the decisive
role in the currency’s path, and the market is often a
bet on how the PBOC’s management of the yuan will
evolve.
That is clear from the lack of volatility in the yuan’s
rate against the dollar. The yuan’s move down against
the dollar in 2022 was a controlled 10 percent move,

and the appreciation over the last 16 months has been
even more controlled crawl.
And the market is clearly massaged with substantial
help from the state banks. In fact that the pattern of
changes in the monthly FX settlement data looks
exactly like the pattern of intervention of a central
bank managing its currency around a tight band (the

### Page 6

6
state banks in practice buy FX to keep the CNY from
testing the strong side of the band, so there is an
asymmetry in how the CNY is managed)
None of this would matter much if China’s economy
was small. But that obviously isn’t the case. Nor would
this matter much if China’s exports didn’t respond to
changes in the RMB’s real (and nominal) value. But
that is also not the case.
There is abundant evidence that China’s exports do
respond to moves in the currency. Periods of RMB

weakness have consistently been associated with
periods of especially rapid Chinese export growth.

### Page 7

7
Chancellor Merz and President Macron got this
right, even if macroeconomic issues haven’t yet
figured prominently in the EU’s dialogue with
China.*** As they note in their open letter to the
European Commission:
“An appreciation of undervalued currencies towards a value
more in line with fundamentals would help support the
rebalancing of trade accounts with the EU in the short and
medium term … European policy discussions focused on
forging a common understanding of these exchange rate issues

and their interaction with global imbalances would be useful.”
The best evidence that exports responded to changes
in the currency, and not simply to changes in the path
of China’s internal demand, is the acceleration in
China’s export growth after the RMB’s depreciation
over the course of 2022.
This is the killer chart.

### Page 8

8
Now, you might ask: what of the acceleration in
export growth in 2020 and 2021? That was pretty
clearly tied to the global increase in goods demand
that came with the pandemic and work from home
requirements. It was expected to fade when the world
economy normalized. But it didn’t. By 2023 Chinese
export growth again topped global trade growth.
The general expectation that the rise in China’s goods
surplus during Covid would prove to be “transitory”

### Page 9

9
heavily influenced the IMF’s work at the time: see the
IMF’s external sector reports of 2023 and 2024.
I would argue that neglecting the exchange rate was
the key reason the IMF’s kept forecasting, incorrectly,
that China’s current account surplus would fall (See
this blog with Shahin Vallee). The IMF certainly
didn’t warn that China’s property downturn and the
RMB’s real depreciation risked a second China shock
in its 2022, 2023, or 2024 surveillance reports.

Concretely, the weaker RMB appears to have
contributed to a very large outperformance of China’s
exports relative to global trade. See the following
chart
That incidentally also happened after the RMB
followed the dollar down in 2003.
In both periods, there were significant structural
shifts. The WTO accession in 2002, and the success

### Page 10

10
of Chinese industrial policies in the “green” industrial
sectors and autos in 2022. In both cses exports were
turbocharged when structural shifts that should have
resulted in a stronger currency were instead amplified
by currency weakness.
No matter. Paul Krugman has noted that a country
that is converging technologically with its trade
partners should see a trend appreciation in its
currency. China got the technological convergence,
but not the real appreciation—at least not until this

year. And the move since the middle of 2025 has
corrected the depreciation of the RMB that occurred
when the RMB followed the dollar down on
liberation day, but not the structural overvaluation.
Now, there is an argument that exchange rate
adjustment is too hot an issue to make it a priority.
The PBOC isn’t interested it seems.
And some believe that in the interest of comity and
good relations with China, the IMF should mirror
the PBOC’s views. After all a stronger RMB would

slow China’s exports and thus slow China’s economy
at a time when China needs export growth to escape
deflation and generate employment. Or so the
argument goes. Concerns about “external balance“
and the negative global spillovers from China’s rising
goods surplus should be ignored and the Fund’s sole
focus should be on policies that lead to internal

### Page 11

11
balance. That provides one rationale for the Fund’s
ongoing decision to essentially ignore the contentious
issue of the PBOC’s ongoing exchange rate
management.
That argument, however, assumes that China’s
economy is small and thus China’s external surplus
can safely be ignored because it isn’t a drag on the
growth of the IMF’s other members.
That clearly isn’t true.
And it isn’t clear that China is more interested in
having a negotiation over its level of basic pension

benefits or the size of its fiscal deficit (where the
IMF continues to make timid recommendations that
would not move the needle—and certainly wouldn’t
meaningfully reduce China’s external surplus) than a
discussion over its currency practices.****
Back in the world of fixed but adjustable exchange
rates, there was an expectation (not always met to be
sure) that surplus countries would allow their
currencies to strengthen to bring their economies

back into external balance, and that other policy tools
would be used to ensure internal balance.
I would argue that China’s exchange rate (against
both the dollar and the basket) has many similarities
to the old world of fixed but adjustable currencies.
True, it moves on a controlled path when it moves

### Page 12

12
between different levels rather than jumping up and
down. But the PBOC does frequently target a level,
and that targeted level does change over time.
I would further argue that steps like the recent tariff
rate quota that will limit China’s exports of hybrid
cars to Europe to around 350,000 units next year (up
from 2025, but well below the 800,000 units of
2026) aren’t going to address the underlying
imbalance on their own. They can provide a bit of a

buffer for specific sectors.
But that buffer, while necessary, won’t address the
broad-based erosion of manufacturing capacity
outside China. This erosion will persist as long as an
undervalued RMB sends the entire world a signal to
use China as its production base.
The new system of managed trade that is emerging in
autos would need to be progressively expanded to an
ever-increasing set of sectors if protection is used as a
substitute for both domestic reform in China and

exchange rate adjustment.
That is now the direction of travel, but it doesn’t
address the underlying systemic issues.
Incidentally, those who believe the path of internal
demand is critical to rebalancing and that exchange
rate moves are secondary need to explain how an
acceleration in domestic demand would, on its own,

### Page 13

13
lead China’s external accounts to adjust. One
important, stylized fact about China is that domestic
demand growth is no longer feeding over to import
growth (ex-gold, that is).
Bottom line: China, which needs a 1.5 percentage
point contribution from net exports every year, is on a
collision course with its trading partners without a
currency correction that provides the basis for more
balanced trade even in the absence of trade
restrictions.
And there isn’t a realistic path towards balance that

doesn’t involve a stronger RMB; there aren’t, as Paul
Krugman once put it, immaculate transfers.
* That downturn shows up in domestic deflationary
pressure, and weak job growth/high rates of (at least
youth) unemployment. China, in other words, isn’t in
“internal balance.” I really liked the report from

### Page 14

14
Kamakshya Trivedi and Hui Shan of Goldman Sachs
arguing China needs two tools to hit two targets
(internal and external balance).
** To be sure, rate differentials matter for exporter
conversion—but so does the expected movement of
the RMB. The market understands this better than
the Fund. The Chen and Suwanapruti (Goldman
Sachs) note uses a framework similar to mine to
describe the CNY’s moves in Q3:
“In our view, sizeable FX inflows have continued to support

RMB appreciation despite unfavorable interest rate
differentials, as strong export receipts and increased exporter
FX conversions have outweighed portfolio outflows. Our
estimated FX conversion ratio rose to 63% in August,
compared with around 45% in normal years, likely reflecting a
shift in market expectations from RMB depreciation to
appreciation.”
*** Š ef č ovi č  has jurisdiction over trade policy
(narrowly defined), but not broader macroeconomic

policies. Europe’s institutional architecture for
exchange rate coordination is not well-developed.
**** The current account surplus is no longer masked
to the same extent as in 2023 and 2024, as SAFE has
fiddled with its BoP methodology once again (per the
Federal Reserve) and the BoP goods surplus is no
longer significantly below the customs goods surplus.
Of course, there are still problems in the data—the
deficit in the investment income account most

《高利高通膨壓抑，留意日股短線震盪加大》

預期。其中，日經225指數在10月初一度上破7萬點
大館，此後因AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正與長端利

日本兩大指數在本月表現也呈現相對分化。日經225
權重權重集中於東京威力科創、愛德萬測試、軟銀集
團等半導體與AI標的，在本波修正幅度較大；相反
的，TOPIX指數因金融、內需與防禦類股權重較高，
加上利率下行有利銀行以外的內需類股，回檔相對溫
和。也間接反映了10月的修正偏向是「AI交易擁擠
的釋放」，而非全面性的景氣衰退定價。
債市方面，10年期日本公債殖利率在10月初一度升

至3.11%、創1999年以來新高，反映日本央行升
息、以及高市內閣財政擴張的雙重疑慮。
此後殖利率一路回落至10月26日的2.82%、下行約
23個基點。這一下行幅度反映全球AI相關信用風險
引發的避險需求。

《日本出口維持韌性，內需動能出現疑慮》
經濟基本面部分，日本9月出口在半導體與AI相關需
求帶動下，連續13個月維持正成長，惟增速放緩至
16.7%、略低於預期；但進口年增卻升至18.9%，主
要因為進口能源價格大漲所致，導致貿易逆差擴大，
外需雖強卻未能轉化為淨出口貢獻。
內需與生產端則收高利高通膨壓力同步降溫。工業生
產增速自年中高點回落，製造業PMI降至七個月來最
低，整體擴張速度已低於上半年水準，細項中新訂單
與出口訂單成長同步放緩，能源與勞動成本則大幅走
升，對企業營運形成壓力。內需改善亦相當有限，零
售銷售下滑、失業率微升、求人求職比回落，反映勞
動市場略有降溫；所幸，實質薪資已連續八個月正成
長、消費者信心小幅回升。

整體而言，日本經濟仍處於「名目成長、實質停滯」
的格局，擴張主要仰賴出口，內需復甦力道偏弱，而
高油價帶來的逆差壓力與生產動能轉弱，將是後續成
長的主要考驗。基於地緣與油價的不確定性，10月
日本央行暫停升息，維持利率不變。

《日銀暫停升息，12月升息預期支撐利率高檔》
日本央行已於9月會期將政策利率上調25個基點至
1.25%，為1995年以來最高 ，也是三個月內第二
次、1990年以來最快的升息節奏。
隨著近期經濟數據，10月份日央如預期暫停升息一
碼，會議同步公布《經濟與物價形勢展望報告》，根
據央行經濟預測顯示潛在通膨「已大致達到2%」。
配合植田和男在10月16日記者會曾表示，若基礎通
膨進一步上升將是「無法接受」的，並在10月19日
國會答詢時表示潛在通膨非常接近2%目標。
加以隨著國際油價再度上破110美元大關，短期政策
重點從「對抗通縮」轉為「防止通膨超標」，是以
12月再度升息一碼的機率已大幅提高。
另一方，高市內閣的食品消費稅減稅與國防支出增

加，使市場對財源安排存疑。不過，10月19日高市
在國會承諾在下一財年預算中妥善管控國債發行規
模，使近期長端利率有所回落。但12月下一財年預
算與國債發行計畫公布時，財政紀律的可信度將再受
檢驗，預期短線利率上行趨緩，維持高檔盤整。

《盤勢觀點-資金輪動加速，防禦成長族群接棒》
10月日本股市震盪、類股表現分化。市場資金明顯
由上半年漲幅龐大的AI硬體與半導體族群撤出，轉向
軟體服務、醫療保健及內需消費等防禦成長型產業。
其中，東京威力科創、SCREEN、瑞薩電子等指標股
普遍出現雙位數跌幅。相較之下，軟體服務類股大漲
17.9%，BayCurrent、SoftBank等個股表現突出。醫
藥生技、消費零售及娛樂觀光相關族群亦受惠於防禦
需求與內需消費韌性而表現相對亦強勢。值得注意的
是，銀行與非銀行金融股同步走弱，多家大型金融機
構跌幅介於8%至15%，顯示市場擔憂金融條件收緊
可能壓抑經濟活動與資本市場表現。
整體而言，10月日股市場風格由「AI硬體主導」轉

向「防禦成長與內需消費主導」，同時對經濟基本面
疑慮小幅升溫，8~9月相對強勢的金融出現回調壓
力。展望未來，短期盤勢仍受利匯率壓力，以及科技
股評價調整影響，加以1H27預估日經225指數企業
獲利年增率轉為負成長，因此本行維持9月以來下調
日股至中性的看法不變，待國際油價回落以及年底年
度預算案底定後，可伺機布局。
重點摘要

《盤勢觀點-澳洲景氣放緩，股匯市轉向盤整》
隨著國際油價維持衝高，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。但在高利率與高通膨
壓力持續積累下對景氣負面壓力逐漸加大。
資料顯示，澳洲9月失業率升至4.72%，已連續兩個
月 落 在 澳 央 主 席 認 為 可 有 效 壓 抑 通 膨 的 失 業 率
4.5~5.0%區間；而通膨方面，9月份澳洲CPI雖由
4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以住房通
膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本輪升息
暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不
振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有

限，加以預期聯準會在12月再度升息，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整。
綜上，持續升息後澳洲基本面轉弱，但高油價壓力又
迫使央行貨幣政策難以轉向寬鬆，加以企業獲利未見
改善動能，澳洲股市難以樂觀，本行維持中性展望。
重點摘要
• 高油價迫使澳洲央行維持偏
鷹態度，但高利率已逐步壓
抑就業與內需。
• 中國需求偏弱限制出口前景，
加上美澳利差優勢收斂，預
期澳幣短期維持區間震盪，
澳股展望偏中性。

## Document 501: トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に ～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリ_1298312e

Source PDF: all report pdf/トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に ～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリ_1298312e.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 23 日

1/3 
トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に 
～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリラ相場や実体経済への波及に警戒～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ トルコ中銀は 10 月 22 日の金融政策委員会において、政策金利(１週間物レポ金利)を 100bp
引き下げ 36.00％とした。利下げは 6 会合ぶりで、金利水準は 2023 年 10 月以来の低さとな
る。背景には、原油高や通貨安への懸念があったにもかかわらず、９月のインフレ率が前年
同月比＋29.73％と 2021 年 11 月以来の低い伸びになるなど、物価の鈍化が着実に進んでい
ることがある。実質金利のプラス幅が拡大して引き締め度合いが強まったため、今回の利下
げは、引き締め姿勢を維持しつつその度合いを調整する従来の動きに沿ったものといえる。 
○ 声明文は過去５会合をほぼ踏襲した。基調的な物価の減速を認める一方、地政学リスクによ
る商品市況の上昇をインフレ見通しの上振れリスクとして警戒し、インフレが著しく悪化し
た場合の再利上げの可能性にも言及しており、「タカ派」姿勢は維持されている。 
○ 金融市場では、投資信託の流動性を巡る問題に加え、MSCI が 10 月15 日にトルコ株の一部指
数変更を暫定凍結し、2027 年6 月までに市場改革の進展が確認できなければ新興国市場から
フロンティア市場への分類変更を協議する方針を示したことから、主要株価指数は下落基調
にある。市場の混乱が長期化すれば、家計の外貨・金への資産移動を通じたリラ安圧力や、
実体経済・政策運営への悪影響が懸念されるため、今後の動向に注意が必要である。

トルコ中央銀行は、10 月 22 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会で、政策金利である１週間物レ
ポ金利を 100bp 引き下げて 36.00％とすることを決定した。同行による利下げ実施は６会合ぶりで
あり、政策金利も2023 年 10 月以来の低水準となる。背景には、同国のインフレ率が着実に鈍化し
ていることがある。

### Page 2

2/3 
イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油高が世
界的なエネルギーインフレを招くなか、
原油需要の大部分を輸入に依存する同
国では、その影響が懸念されてきた。さ
らに、原油高は輸入額の増加による対外
収支の悪化を招いており、リラの対ドル
相場はジリ安の展開が続く。そのため、
輸入物価の押し上げもインフレ圧力を
増幅させることが懸念された。しかし、
直近９月のインフレ率は前年同月比＋
29.73％と 2021 年 11 月以来の低い伸び
となった（図１）。 
同行は 2024 年 12 月に現在の経済チー
ムの下で初の利下げに踏み切り、その後
も実質金利をプラスに維持する抑制的
なスタンスを維持しながら、断続的な利
下げに動いてきた。このところはインフ
レ率の鈍化を受けて、実質金利のプラス
幅が拡大するなど引き締め度合いが強
まる動きが確認されていた（図２）。この
ため、今回の利下げ決定は、引き締め姿
勢を維持しつつ、その度合いを調整して
きた動きに沿ったものと捉えられる。 
会合後に公表した声明文では、物価動向について「足元の動きや先行指標は基調的な減速が続い
ていることを裏付けている」との見方を示した。そのうえで「経済指標や供給ショックによる物価
への影響が限定的なものにとどまることは内需の弱さを反映している」とした。一方で、「地政学リ
スクによる商品市況の上昇はインフレ見通しの上振れリスクになる」として、「地政学リスクが物
価動向、経済活動、インフレ期待に与える影響を注視する」と、前回会合と同じ見方を示した。 
先行きの政策運営について「需要動向やリラ相場、インフレ期待を通じた物価安定を実現するま
で引き締めスタンスを維持する」との従来からの姿勢を維持した。具体的な政策についても「イン
フレ見通しと実績、基調的な動きを勘案して決定する」、「会合ごとにインフレ見通しを注視しつつ
慎重に見直す」とし、「インフレ見通しが著しく、かつ持続的に悪化した場合は引き締め姿勢を強め
る」、「インフレの上振れリスクに引き続き高い注意を払っている」と、引き続き再利上げに動く可
能性をあらためて強調した。 
また、「信用市場や預金市場で予期せぬ事態が発生した場合は、流動性を巡る状況を注視しつつ、
マクロプルーデンス政策を強化する」と従来からの考えを繰り返した。そのうえで、「インフレ目標

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
の実現に向けて、入手可能なデータに基づく透明性の高い枠組みの下で必要な環境整備を図るべく
政策決定を行う」との考えをあらためて示している。声明文の内容は、過去５会合をほぼ踏襲して
いるうえ、過去４会合と同様にインフレの上振れリスクに対する警戒感を強調しており、「タカ派」
姿勢を維持している。 
一方で、トルコ金融市場では、主要株価指数であるイスタンブール 100 指数（BIST100）が下落基
調をたどるなど、不安定な動きが続いている（注１）（図３）。背景には、一部の投資信託の流動性
問題をきっかけとする売り圧力に加え、
指数算出会社の MSCI が 10 月 15 日、ト
ルコ株式に関する一部の指数変更を暫
定的に凍結し、2027 年６月までに十分な
市場改革の進展が確認できなければ、新
興国市場からフロンティア市場などへ
の分類変更に向けた協議を検討する方
針を示したことがある（注２）。こうした
市場の混乱は家計部門のリスク回避姿
勢を強め、外貨や金などへの資産移動を
通じてリラ相場の下押し圧力につなが
る可能性もある。市場の混乱が長期化すれば、実体経済への悪影響が顕在化するとともに、政府の
政策運営にも影響が及ぶおそれがあり、先行きはその動向に注意を払う必要性が高まっている。 
以 上
